1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any sorting module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 613. The projective resolution has length at most 157 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to sorting theory, we decompose the problem into 613 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 157. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The sorting operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 629. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 946 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the sorting space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 629 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 946. QED.
